Kullanıcı bu depoda "sistem kur", "özellik ekle", satış/davranış/öneri/panel/grafik/ödeme/hesap gibi bir şey istediğinde MUTLAKA bu beceriyi yükle — kullanıcı beceriyi adıyla anmasa bile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-differential-methylation-testing))

- **beta-binomial-enriched-window-testing**: Use when analyzing eCLIP/CLIP-seq FASTQs that have matched input controls and at least 2 CLIP replicates, and you need reproducible, annotated binding-site windows rather than peak calls.
